### ✓ 2026-09-08: `topics/arguments-against-materialism` states four claims its own cited sources explicitly disclaim — the survey strips the disciplines the detail articles installed
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/arguments-against-materialism.md
- **Notes**: **ONE PATTERN, FOUR LOCI, ALL IN-PLACE REQUALIFICATIONS — do not rewrite, re-argue, or re-cite.** The article is a survey that points at detail articles; in four places it imports a claim from a Map article that explicitly disclaims the stronger reading, and states the stronger reading. **Every counter-quote below is verbatim and line-verified on disk 2026-09-08.** This is invisible from inside the file, which is why eleven deep-reviews did not surface it: each sentence is well-formed, correctly linked and correctly attributed, and the defect is only legible when the *target* is opened. Fix by matching each pointer to its source's own language.
  **LOCUS 1 — L57.** Survey: *"physicalist theories themselves **independently** narrowing toward dualist-compatible positions through their own defensive modifications."* Source `concepts/concession-convergence` **L121**: *"Within Western analytic philosophy the convergence is strong but **not independent in the sense the inference requires**; across traditions it is weak."* The word "independently" is the whole defect — the source scores this convergence as *within*-tradition and non-independent. Drop or requalify it. Word-neutral.
  **LOCUS 2 — L63.** Survey: *"The pattern is reinforced by cross-traditional convergence—Indian, Islamic, African, and Buddhist philosophical traditions reaching structurally similar conclusions through methods independent of Western philosophy of mind."* Source `topics/cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility` **L142**: *"It is tempting to read these as three independent confirmations. **They are not.**"* and **L146**: *"**Madhyamaka reads the convergence as *dissolving* the substantial-witness reading rather than confirming it**… the further selection runs on separately-supported arguments and does not inherit the convergence's evidential weight."* ⚠️ **Sharper than a wording fix**: the survey counts *the unity of consciousness* among its independent arguments (L57, L59, L119) — the argument whose premise Madhyamaka denies — while recruiting Buddhist traditions as convergent support. `tenets.md` already registers the Madhyamaka no-self analysis as *"a genuine bedrock disagreement, not an in-framework defect the Map can refute"*; the survey inherits none of that. The words *no-self*, *Madhyamaka*, *anattā*, *emptiness* appear **nowhere** in the article (grep exit 1). Install the source's own caveat: the convergence earns *irreducibility*, not the Map's specific reading. ~15 words.
  **LOCUS 3 — L99, and this one is also a Tenet 5 breach.** Survey: *"For the positive argument that parsimony actually ***favours*** interactionist dualism, see [parsimony-case-for-interactionist-dualism](/topics/parsimony-case-for-interactionist-dualism/)."* Source **L39**: *"interactionist dualism is **at least as parsimonious as** physicalism"*; source **L41**: *"**The Map does not endorse the inference *simpler-therefore-truer* here.** The fifth tenet … holds … that self-binding applies to parsimony arguments *for* dualism exactly as it applies to those *against* it … **this article only removes a barrier**."* The target is scrupulous in its second paragraph; the survey's one-sentence summary is where the discipline is lost. And `tenets.md` **L147** rules out by name *"—internally—any Map argument that leans on parsimony as if this tenet did not apply to it"* (**L145**: *"The discipline is symmetric: parsimony cannot decide for or against a framework"*). The same paragraph invokes the Occam's-limits tenet to disarm the *materialist's* parsimony argument two sentences earlier, so the asymmetry is self-contained and visible on the page. **Two-word fix**: "favours" → "is at least as parsimonious as", plus a half-clause noting the self-binding cuts both ways. Near word-neutral. *Secondary, light touch only*: L97's *"parsimony arbitrates between theories of equal explanatory power. A 'simpler' theory that fails to explain the data has not earned the parsimony discount"* keeps parsimony alive as an arbiter-with-a-precondition two sentences before citing a tenet that denies it is a reliable arbiter here at all. Do not rewrite the section for this.
  **LOCUS 4 — L129–133, §"The Self-Undermining Problem". The strongest finding.** Survey asserts flatly *"Physical causes are not responsive to logical norms"* (bare; a corpus grep finds this sentence **once**, here, unsupported anywhere) and concludes materialism *"**cannot be rationally held**—that the position, if true, destroys the conditions for rational belief in itself."* It offers exactly one materialist response (reliabilism) and dismisses it. Source `topics/argument-from-reason` **L129**: *"The Anscombe lineage matures … into non-reductive physicalism—**the live opponent the argument must actually engage**"* (Davidson's anomalous monism and Yablo's proportionality account get a full sympathetic hearing there). Source **L133**: *"**This is not a decisive disproof.** The non-reductive physicalist has replies … The honest verdict is that the argument **converts Anscombe's compatibilism into a real dilemma rather than dissolving it**."* **Anscombe, Davidson, Yablo and Kim appear nowhere in the survey.** Minimum fix: hedge the bare premise, name non-reductive physicalism as the live opponent with a pointer, and downgrade "cannot be rationally held" to the detail article's "real dilemma" verdict. ~40 words — the largest of the four.
  **LOCUS 5 (SEPARATE ISSUE, MEDIUM) — L67, altered-state symmetry.** This file is the **only live symmetry flag corpus-wide** (`get_symmetry_flags` returns exactly 1). ⚠️ **Read the grading carefully: the double-counting the audit exists to catch is ABSENT and must not be "fixed".** L67 already aggregates the four supportive cases into *"a suggestive **pattern**"* (singular) of *"modest evidential weight compared to the philosophical arguments above"*, names the evidential grade (*"case reports rather than controlled studies"*) and volunteers the deflationary alternatives. That is the substance of "the cluster carries the evidential weight of one" in different words. **Do NOT install a symmetry section and do NOT pad toward the marker vocabulary.** What *is* missing: **the audit's disruptive-cluster pass is a FALSE pass.** Its `disruptive_clusters: ['dementia']` comes entirely from the word *neurodegeneration* occurring twice **inside the terminal-lucidity sentence**, where it is the substrate of a *supportive* case. A grep for every disruptive term (anaesthesia/propofol/ketamine/xenon, slow-wave/dreamless sleep/NREM, brain damage/injury/lesion/TBI, vegetative state/PVS/MCS, dementia/Alzheimer's) returns those two words and nothing else — **zero disruptive cases are cited as challenges**. The one near-miss, *covert consciousness*, is recruited on the supportive side. Concrete cost: L67 says terminal lucidity *"challenges the monotonic neural-substrate-to-cognitive-capacity relationship that production models predict"* without noting that anaesthesia, brain damage and PVS are the mass of evidence **establishing** that relationship, and that filter framing accommodates them by interface- rather than generator-degradation. **One clause inside the existing sentence**, on the pattern of `concepts/altered-states-of-consciousness` **L77** (*"A sophisticated production theorist would note that propofol and ketamine act on different receptor systems … so production theory **does** predict different phenomenal outcomes"*). ~25 words.
  **LOWER-SEVERITY, OPTIONAL — two internal tensions, one clause each.** (a) L119 *"they **rule out** the position that dominates the field"* vs L123 four lines later *"conditions that could **in principle** be met"* — a position that could be vindicated is disfavoured, not ruled out; L123 is the honest one. (b) L139 *"the materialist **consensus**"* vs the article's own L85 figure of *"roughly 52%"* — a bare majority the article itself supplies. "majority" fixes it.
  **STRENGTHS — PRESERVE, DO NOT TOUCH**: **L87** is a genuine steelman of physicalism and the best paragraph in the article (*"This persistence is not irrational … many judge that the theoretical costs of abandoning materialism outweigh the costs of living with the explanatory gap"*); **L61–63** anticipates and partly concedes the strongest objection to its own central move; **L121–125** is real falsifiability work naming three defeaters plus a separate empirical defeater for the Map's own mechanism; **L67's** evidential discipline (see Locus 5); **L79's** Napoleonic-wars analogy; **L115's** treatment of eliminativism as limit case rather than strawman.
  **LENGTH — measured 2026-09-08, do not quote from memory.** `analyze_length` → 2936 words, `status: ok`, topics thresholds **soft 3000 / hard 4000 / critical 6000**. Only **64 words** to soft but **1064** to hard, and this file carries a heavy reference apparatus, so re-run `analyze_length` after editing rather than assuming. The four discipline fixes total ~55-80 words and three of them are swaps; crossing into `soft_warning` is acceptable here given the hard-limit headroom, but prefer in-place requalification over addition, and piped wikilinks (`[[slug|existing text]]`) cost zero words if a pointer is needed.
  **NOT IN SCOPE — do not mint or edit.** The four source articles are each **exemplary about their own limits**; the defect is in the survey, not in them. Do not "fix" `concession-convergence`, `cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility`, `parsimony-case-for-interactionist-dualism` or `argument-from-reason`. Also **not** in scope: the counting question raised in the review's Counterarguments section (the survey defends independence for ~5 of the 9 arguments it counts at L57 while the "remarkable coincidence" inference leans on the headline count) — that is a genuine finding but a structural rewrite, deliberately left untasked. Sync both trees afterwards and bump `ai_modified`; fork edits do not sync and the published page keeps the defect until pre-push.

### ✓ 2026-09-08: Update `topics/bacterial-chemotaxis-and-minimal-biogenic-cognition` citations — the "parked" noise-vs-choice dispute has a published 2025 rejoinder, and a 2026 cognitive-spectrum rival the article's two-school map has no slot for
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/bacterial-chemotaxis-and-minimal-biogenic-cognition.md
- **Notes**: From literature-drift audit 2026-09-08 (Audit One of [calibration-audit-triple](/project/calibration-audit-triple/)). **Two of three drift checks fired; read the first one's caveat before acting on it.** *Check 1 (median-year lag)* — 9 dated external references, median year **2006**, i.e. **20 years** behind `ai_modified` 2026-08-07, against `median_year_lag_threshold: 5`. ⚠️ **This check is largely a FALSE signal here and must NOT be "fixed": the pre-2020 citations are the canonical primary sources and are correct** — Berg & Brown 1972 (*Nature* 239:500–504, the founding three-dimensional-tracking measurement), Macnab & Koshland 1972 (*PNAS*, the gradient-sensing mechanism), Koshland 1977 (*Science* 196:1055–1063, the response-regulator model), Berg 2004 (*E. coli in Motion*, the standard monograph), and Lyon 2006/2015 (the biogenic-approach papers that define the article's frame). **Do not replace, modernise, or drop any of these**; a 1972 citation for the founding measurement is a primary source, not a stale one. *Check 2 (missing topically-appropriate work)* — **this is the real signal, and it is corroborated independently: `grep -n "2025"` on the article returns ZERO hits, and every `2026` hit is frontmatter or the Map self-cite.** The newest external citation in the article is Robinson et al. 2024. Two verified, genuinely uncited (surname-grepped: 0 hits for `Nesin`, `Chandrankunnel`, `Dodig`, `Crnkovic`), topically-appropriate papers, both metadata-verified at Crossref this run with full author lists:
  1. **Nesin, S. M., & Chandrankunnel, M. (2025). "The need for a new perspective on decision-making in bacteria." *Communicative & Integrative Biology* 18(1):2463926. doi:10.1080/19420889.2025.2463926** (review article, 2025-02-12) — **the highest-value addition, because it lands exactly on the question the article deliberately parks.** L74 quotes the *EMBO Reports* charge that the bacterial cell does not "make a choice" and that apparent decision "reduces to stochastic differences in protein levels"; L76 then says "The Map does not need to settle the empirical dispute between CBC and its critics about whether bacterial 'decision' is real proto-choice or protein-level noise." That dispute has since **moved**: this paper is the published CBC-side rejoinder to that specific criticism, arguing behavioural flexibility arises from **multiple pathways with convergence and divergence** (an explicit brain analogy) rather than from protein-level noise. The article can still park the adjudication — but it currently presents the deflationary reading as the last word spoken, which is no longer accurate.
  2. **Dodig-Crnkovic, G. (2026). "De-anthropomorphizing the mind: life as a cognitive spectrum in a unified framework for biological minds." *Frontiers in Systems Neuroscience* 20:1730097. doi:10.3389/fnsys.2026.1730097** (2026-01-22) — supplies a **third position the article's two-school map has no slot for.** The article frames the field as a binary: Lyon, who keeps cognition and consciousness *separable* (L66), versus CBC, which makes them *coterminous* (L70). Dodig-Crnkovic's info-computational (ICON) account instead treats cognition, sentience, awareness and mind as one **graded spectrum** present "from the onset of life" and progressively more integrated with biological organisation — neither cleanly separable nor cleanly coterminous. A live 2026 intermediate position is a genuine gap in the article's map of the rivals.
  **Considered and EXCLUDED as frame-divergent** (recorded so it is not re-proposed): Vitas, M. (2025). "Towards a Possible Definition of Consciousness." *BioSystems* 254:105526, doi:10.1016/j.biosystems.2025.105526 — a definitional/emergentist proposal ("Consciousness is an emergent property of a far from equilibrium system of quantum particles sustained by an autopoietic system") claiming to solve the hard problem. It engages neither the chemotaxis mechanism nor the competency-without-experience frame, and pulls toward an emergentism the Map rejects on other grounds. **Do not add it to reach a count.** *Check 3 (empirical-record currency)* — **clean**: `find_superlative_claims` returns **0**, independently confirmed by hand. The article's superlatives ("the sharpest case", "the lowest rung the ladder has", "one of the most completely characterised") are framework-internal comparatives, not supersedable record claims. Nothing to verify on this channel.
  **Action and guardrails.** Integrate the two papers into the CBC section (L68–76) and the biogenic section (L60–66) respectively; the natural home for (1) is alongside the Robinson et al. dissent it answers, and for (2) a sentence distinguishing spectrum accounts from both Lyon's separability and CBC's coterminism. ⚠️ **Both new papers cut AGAINST the article's deflationary line** — (1) defends bacterial decision-making as more than noise, (2) grades sentience continuously down to first life. Represent them at full strength rather than as foils; the article's own standard (L72, "It deserves statement at full strength rather than caricature") applies. ⚠️ **Do NOT inflate evidential calibration on the strength of more citations** — see [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/). The bacterial rung sits at the *speculative-integration floor* (L86) and adding two citations that run against the Map's verdict is a reason to leave that placement alone, not to move it in either direction. The framework-relative verdict at L82 and the "no interface where no substrate" allocation rule at L84 are unaffected by either paper and should not be restated or strengthened. ⚠️ **Do not touch reference 10** — `Southgate, A. & Oquatre-huit, C.` is the house AI-pseudonym convention (Opus 4.8), not a fabricated author; this has been raised as a false alarm before. **Length budget is ample**: 2452 words against soft 3000 / hard 4000, so ~500 words are affordable without a condense pass — but prefer piped wikilinks for any new cross-references so reciprocals cost zero words. Consider whether [single-cell-proto-agency-and-the-evidence-problem](/topics/single-cell-proto-agency-and-the-evidence-problem/), which owns the noise-vs-choice question this article parks, is the better home for (1) — if so, place it there and add one sentence here pointing at it.

The retracted claim survives in three places, verified this run. **(1) LIVE LOCUS — `topics/primary-secondary-quality-boundary` L79**: "That strongest reading is a composite the Map assembles rather than **a view defended in print**". Note the wording differs from the corrected article's, so an exact-string sweep for "not a position anyone defends in print" returns **zero** here — search the claim, not the string. **(2) TASK FENCE, the urgent one — the open P3 on `concepts/reductionism`** (todo header at approx L2060) carries in its notes a calibration fence marked "**do not weaken any**": "the rival is a **composite the Map constructed**, not a position anyone defends in print ... That fence will write the corrected-away claim into new prose when the task runs. **Amend the fence, do not delete it** - its length and reciprocal-link content is sound and independently useful; only the "nobody defends it" clause is retracted. **(3) ⚠️ AND THE FENCE'S OWN EVIDENCE IS MISREAD.** "Lyre disavows the association verbatim" is true of the *words* and false of the *sense*, re-verified in the Europe PMC raw XML (PMC9396309) on 2026-09-07: Lyre declines the label *structural realism* because SR is a doctrine about "the theoretical entities posited by our most successful and mature scientific theories ... primarily the fundamental theories in physics", and then writes "**there seems to be a family resemblance to neurophenomenal structuralism, since our doctrine denies phenomenal intrinsicality**". He declines "realism about the phenomenal" because he holds something *more deflationary*: an exclusion argument on which "the **Q-structure plays no causal role at all**", leaving him "consistent with a **reduction of the phenomenal to the neural**". So the quote's fidelity was verified while its reading was inverted - quote-fidelity and quote-reading are orthogonal. Any surviving use must say what Lyre declines and why. **Scope**: fix locus (1), amend the fence in (2), and correct the Lyre gloss wherever it appears. `primary-secondary-quality-boundary` is 3022w against a topics 4000 hard ceiling, so there is room. Do NOT edit `concepts/ontic-structural-realism` (corrected 2026-09-07) or the research note.

Replace with a graded statement.   **(6a) Calibration-virtue inflation — the genuinely new finding, and the strongest.** The article does not merely disclose that LIS is not evidence; it banks the disclosure as trust. Two loci, both grep-verified: §The Honest Ledger — *"a model that can say clearly where its own most striking argument does not reach earns more credit than one that reads every dissociation as a win"* — and, same section, *"Far from weakening the account, the concession is what makes it trustworthy"*. ### ✓ 2026-09-07: `voids/modality-void` — the lead premise is denied by its own cited source, the claim to undercut the cross-modal apex contradicts the article's first-personal qualification, and the common-cause null is named without being run
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/modality-void.md
- **Notes**: ⚠️ **LENGTH IS AT CEILING — this is a REPLACEMENT-ONLY pass.** Printed from `tools.curate.length.THRESHOLDS`: `voids` = 2000 soft / 3000 hard / 4000 critical. `analyze_length` read **1998 words** — two under soft. Re-measure before planning; do not add prose except where item (3) requires it, and pay for that with the deletions in (2), (5) and (6). Target net-neutral or slightly negative. Eight findings, three of them structural rather than presentational. **(1) HIGH — the lead premise is the negation of Fréchette (2023), the article's own "cleanest exhibit."** The lead says modality arrives as *format*, "never as a separate datum beside the content where a check could reach it." Fréchette's actual thesis (verified verbatim in the Europe PMC full text, PMC10946872): *"the basic experiential fact about Q is twofold: We not only subsume the content of our experiences under different quality kinds but also subsume tokens of our experiences under different experience kinds, or senses."* A subsumption of the token under an experience-kind IS a separate datum. Worse, §The Counting Face rests a horn on Nudds's "it's not clear that the distinction has any first-personal significance: I don't think it matters to Alice which way she perceives the vase" — and Fréchette quotes that sentence **in order to reject it**, writing eleven words later *"In contrast to Nudds ... it does matter to Alice which way she perceives the vase"* (same source, verified). The article endorses Nudds in one section and Nudds's rejector in the next. **FIX**: adopt the reliabilist formulation — the tag is present and evidently given, what is missing is a second channel able to audit the first. In-place replacement of the lead clause; rewrite the **McGurk** paragraph so Fréchette's positive thesis is absorbed as a concession the void survives (the tag is evident, which is *why* the false tag is undetectable) rather than presented as an exhibit for its negation; repair §The Counting Face by dropping the Nudds horn or marking that Fréchette rejects it. **Do NOT re-verify the quotes as fabricated — all four check out verbatim; the defect is the inference, not the quotation.** **(2) HIGH — DELETE the sentence "It undercuts the *method*: a division indexed by modality from the mind side imports a carving the mind cannot verify."** (§Relation to Site Perspective, Dualism). It contradicts the article's own second paragraph ("the limit is on what a mind can learn about its own channels, not on what can be learned about them") — a limit that concedes third-person recovery cannot undercut a third-person method. It also mis-describes its target: `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` indexes the division from the **brain** side (V1 retinotopy, cochlear frequency decomposition, ~400 olfactory receptor types, mechanoreceptor populations) and already carries its own scope condition plus its own common-cause self-audit. Deleting frees ~20 words. **⚠️ DO NOT EDIT THE APEX — out of scope for this task; the apex observation is recorded in the review as prose only.** **(3) HIGH — run the common-cause null instead of naming it.** The closing clause "Calibrated per the Map's [common-cause-null](/project/common-cause-null/), the void locates a residue and evidences no account of one" restates the evidential-status point one level down (and is adapted from `voids/fusion-void` L40), not the common-cause point. The three attribution demonstrations — oral referral, thermal grill, McGurk — share one upstream mechanism (central/multisensory integration assigning a channel label that mismatches the transducer) read through one introspective channel: one observation read three times. **[P-M2](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m2)** (`positions/methodology-and-calibration`, credence *high*) makes the discount a standing obligation, "not only to the void catalogue," and grants the stronger reading "only by naming and passing the distinguishing test"; **[P-V1](/positions/voids-as-evidence/#p-v1)** (`positions/voids-as-evidence`) is the inherited void-catalogue instance. **FIX** (~40 words, the only genuine addition): name the shared upstream, state that the three carry the weight of one pattern, and either name a distinguishing test or record that none is available. The article's one real partial defence is that the three *faces* sit at different levels of description (taxonomic count / token mis-attribution / routing fact) where the three *demonstrations* do not — claim that, not more. **(4) MEDIUM — no three-kinds classification for any of the three faces.** `voids/three-kinds-of-void` is explicit that the kind attaches per face ("It is not the void that falls into a kind but the void's *territory* ... its different faces sit at different points on the unexplored / unexplorable / occluded axis") and its own worked case gives one void three faces and three classifications; it also notes palette extension's verification face is "a candidate *unexplorable*" while its induction face shrinks. Siblings classify (fusion, source-attribution, palette-extension, perceptual-reality-monitoring all carry the vocabulary). This article names three faces and classifies none; its only remark concedes the counting face is *unexplored*. **FIX**: install per-face classification as a replacement for the vague "is granted, and is why the void is stated first-personally" clause in §Two Deflations, at roughly current length. Defensible on the article's own evidence: counting = *unexplored* (conceded); attribution = *occluded* on (1)'s reformulation (the only channel that could check the tag is the channel that produced it); boundary = *unexplored* third-personally, *occluded* first-personally. **(1) sets this** — the "no datum" wording would point at *unexplorable*, which the evidence does not reach. **(5) MEDIUM — the thermal grill gloss contradicts Craig & Bushnell.** The verbatim quote is correct (verified, PMID 8023144). The gloss "the delivered class is nociceptive while the stimulated channels are innocuous and thermal" is false on the same abstract: recordings came from "two classes of ascending spinothalamic tract neurons that are sensitive to innocuous **or noxious** cold," and the model is "central disinhibition, or **unmasking**" — unmasking releases the nociceptive channel, so it IS active. The *stimulus* is innocuous; the channels are not. The preceding "cold-sensitive and pain-sensitive spinothalamic channels" also mis-renders two classes that are both cold-responsive, differing in innocuous vs noxious tuning. **This weakens the exhibit**: if genuine nociceptive activity delivers the burn, the channel tag is correct and the error is about tissue state — a veridicality error, not a channel mis-attribution. **FIX**: correct both sentences, then re-evaluate whether the grill belongs in the attribution face at all; demotion to a one-sentence near-case shortens the section and helps pay for (3). **(6) MEDIUM — the strongest deflation is raised after the deflations section and never answered.** §Approaches to the Edge, Indirect methods: "if the count is a neurobiological matter, first-person access to it is not merely unreliable but irrelevant." §Two Deflations answers conventionality and the empirical count, not irrelevance — and if first-person access is *irrelevant*, the counting face is a dissolution, not a void. It is also an over-concession running against the Map's own first-person commitments, and larger than Keeley (cited at second hand via O'Callaghan 2012, honestly disclosed in the reference list) supports. **FIX, cheapest**: cut back to "non-authoritative rather than merely unreliable"; frees words. Answering it as a third deflation needs headroom the article does not have. **(7) LOW–MEDIUM — word-neutral replacement.** "a consciousness selecting among physically underdetermined outcomes would select over contents whose channel it cannot read" mis-states Tenet 3, which commits to *outcome-selection* ("influence over which definite outcome becomes actual") and explicitly registers-but-declines the Process-1 relocation because it "would weaken outcome-selection to context-setting." Recast so selection operates on physical outcomes whose *downstream* content carries the unreadable tag. Honest "An angle, not a finding" flag caps severity but does not excuse mis-stating the committed mechanism. **(8) LOW–MEDIUM — three absolutes on three cases.** "None exposes the tag itself, and no subject can audit contents no manipulation has touched" (§Direct methods), "never as a separate datum" (lead), "Attending harder answers none of them" (§Counting Face). Prefer supplying the principle over hedging: on (1)'s reformulation the argument is short and already sanctioned — any audit of the tag is performed by the mechanism that assigned it, the same structure `positions/agency-and-will` [P-A4](/positions/agency-and-will/#p-a4) accepts for `voids/agency-void` ("every such check is performed by the faculty whose causal status is in question"). This also supplies the *occluded* classification (4) needs, so (1), (4) and (8) should be executed as one coherent move. **PRESERVE VERBATIM**: the entire reference list and every quotation (11/11 references verified at Crossref/PubMed, 10 quotations verified verbatim against raw sources — Macpherson ×5, Fréchette ×2, Nudds ×1 nested, von Melchner ×2, Craig & Bushnell ×1, Liang ×1); the two secondary-source disclosures in refs 5 and 8; the two-qualification lead structure; the "Consonant, not probative — a physicalist reads the same signature as late convergence..." hedge; §Distinguishing Sibling Voids (its fusion-void and palette-extension-void characterisations both check out). ATTRIBUTION: append model to `ai_system` in the plus-joined form if composing prose; `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`. Mirror into `hugo/content/voids/modality-void.md` — run sync and grep BOTH trees before reporting done.

### ✓ 2026-09-07: `concepts/self-stultification` leans on a lever that has a named published rebuttal it never engages
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/self-stultification.md
- **Notes**: From the 2026-09-07 Claude Opus 5 outer review, §4.9 — the one finding in that review that names genuinely new territory rather than an article-level defect, and the only one minted as its own task. The corpus's anti-epiphenomenalist self-stultification argument is load-bearing across **96 live files**, and `concepts/self-stultification.md` currently answers only the Externalist Reply and the illusionism convergence. It never engages the standing published dissolution. **Verified at Crossref during collection**: Helen Yetter-Chappell, "Dualism all the way down: why there is no paradox of phenomenal judgment", *Synthese* 200(2), DOI `10.1007/s11229-022-03654-6`. ⚠️ **Two reviewer errors not to propagate.** The review dates it **2023**; the publisher of record says **2022** — cite 2022. And the review glosses it as a "double-edge" that "constrains interactionist as well as epiphenomenalist readings"; it does no such thing. The paper is a *defence of epiphenomenalism*, arguing from the abstract that "there is nothing paradoxical or problematic about the epiphenomenalist's understanding of phenomenal judgments or phenomenal self-knowledge", the appearance of paradox arising "from inconsistently combining (epiphenomenalist) dualism about qualia with a physicalistic conception of subjects of experience" — i.e. the epiphenomenalist escapes by declining a physicalist conception of *subjects*, not by constraining interactionism. So the correct framing is narrower and more useful than the reviewer's: the Map's lever has a live, named answer in the literature, and the page should meet it on its own terms rather than absorb an overstated double-edge. Scope: engage the 2022 dissolution in `concepts/self-stultification.md` — state Yetter-Chappell's move (dualism about subjects as well as qualia), say what it costs an epiphenomenalist who takes it, and say whether the Map's self-stultification argument survives against an opponent who pays that price. Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. Dedupe checked at mint: Yetter-Chappell has **zero** occurrences corpus-wide, "paradox of phenomenal judgment" appears in exactly one live file (`concepts/philosophical-zombies` §"The Paradox of Phenomenal Judgment", a section heading), and no open P0–P2 task targets `concepts/self-stultification.md`. Consider a reciprocal pointer from the zombies section once the engagement lands.

### ✓ 2026-09-07: `topics/ethics-under-dualism` L82 claims agent causation eliminates causal luck — the apex it depends on says the opposite, and the apex is right
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/ethics-under-dualism.md
- **Notes**: Live cross-article contradiction, both sides verified verbatim on disk 2026-09-06. `topics/ethics-under-dualism` **L82**: "The [implications](/topics/moral-implications-of-genuine-agency/) extend to obligation, character formation as genuine self-creation through accumulated selections, and **the elimination of causal luck**." `apex/moral-architecture-of-consciousness` **L94**, naming the *same* companion article as its source: "The implications explored in the [companion article](/topics/moral-implications-of-genuine-agency/)—obligation addressing open possibilities, character as self-creation, **moral luck relocated rather than eliminated**—are **available on either metaphysics**." Two articles cite one companion and report opposite conclusions from it.
  **The apex is right and the fix is one clause.** Even an agent capable of irreducible choice does not choose the conditions under which choosing occurs — temperament, early development, available information, encountered temptations, bodily state, social circumstance. Libertarian freedom relocates responsibility's grounding; it does not erase those asymmetries. Replace "the elimination of causal luck" with the apex's formulation. **Read `topics/moral-implications-of-genuine-agency` first** to confirm which reading it actually supports before editing either citing article — it is the common source and neither citing file should be trusted over it.
  **A second, related overclaim in the same section, flagged by the reviewer's §8 and worth checking in the same pass**: metaphysical origination does not by itself establish that a wrongdoer *deserves suffering*. A theory of agency and a theory of punishment are distinct; one can accept genuine authorship while favouring protection, restoration, or constrained blame. If L82's surrounding prose slides from "desert would acquire metaphysical grounding" to a retributive conclusion, mark the gap. Note the apex's L94 already models the careful version — it credits sophisticated compatibilists (Frankfurt, Fischer and Ravizza, Wolf) with metaphysically substantive desert-grounding capacities rather than treating compatibilism as mere pragmatic convention, and L82's "from pragmatic convention into metaphysical fact" is the uncorrected form of exactly that contrast. Length-neutral; `ai_modified` live; sync and grep both trees.

If the range genuinely needs sourcing against Tegmark, that is a separate question to settle *after* the arithmetic is right.

- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/consciousness-and-causal-powers.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-09-07 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), all six independently verified — see the review's Verification Notes for the evidence and for the four charges that were REFUTED (do not act on those). Fix in ONE pass to avoid same-file churn. (1) **Arithmetic**: three loci say the Zeno gap is "twelve to sixteen orders of magnitude" — ⚠️ **one of the three is the HYPHENATED compound form `twelve-to-sixteen-orders-of-magnitude`, so a grep on the spaced form finds only two and leaves the third live** (driver-verified: 3 in this file, 5 corpus-wide); re-derived from the page's own $10^{-13}$–$10^{-20}$ s coherence range against 10–30 Hz attention the answer is **twelve to nineteen**, and the companion "$\geq 10^{13}$ Hz" understates the fast end, which needs $\geq 10^{20}$ Hz. (2) **Stranded dependent**: "No new energy, no thermodynamic violations… selection among energy-conserving alternatives" is contradicted by `concepts/conservation-laws-and-mental-causation`, revised 2026-09-04 *after* this page, which says the alternatives need not be energy-degenerate and that ensemble-mean preservation does *not* establish thermodynamic innocence (open extractable-work debt). Replace the categorical assurance with the constraint. (3) **Inverted grounding**: the ethics paragraph argues from causal efficacy to intrinsic value and says it "grounds" `topics/phenomenal-value-realism`, which in fact grounds value in phenomenal character by identity. Say causal efficacy connects phenomenal value to behaviour and selection; do not make it the ground. (4) **Over-concession**: Nisbett and Wilson (1977) established limited introspective *access*, not that the behaviour was not "consciously initiate[d]" — re-frame, do not delete. (5) **Denominator**: the Bodien **25% figure is CORRECT and must not be changed** (verified at Europe PMC: 60 of 241, 25%, from a convenience sample of 353); what is wrong is framing the denominator as "unresponsive wakefulness" when the 241 were mixed diagnostic categories lacking observable command-following. Also soften "strongest clinical exhibit against epiphenomenalism" toward the specialist page's own "coherence rather than compulsion" grade. (6) **Strawman**: the identity-theory "does all the work" paragraph misrepresents identity theory as positing a phenomenal item alongside a neural cause; it holds that the two are one event under two descriptions. Apply the direct-refutation discipline — identify whether the reply available is a defect on the opponent's own terms, an unsupported foundational move, or an honest framework-boundary disagreement, and write it in natural journal-quality prose. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body**; the classification belongs in the changelog entry. Also worth doing while in here: §"Sustained Attention" still leads with Zeno where `positions/quantum-interface` [P-Q1](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q1) prefers post-decoherence selection. **Folded in 2026-09-07 from the same-cycle Claude Opus 5 outer review, which audited this same article** — all article-level, all verified this run, all belonging in the same single pass; the review's site-wide charges were refuted and must not be acted on (see that review's Verification Notes). (7) **Stapp inversion, article-level**: §"Pattern amplification" and §"Sustained Attention" recruit `[[concepts/stapp-quantum-mind]]` toward outcome-biasing ("consciousness influences which collapses into actuality") while the article never says "Process 1". The origin node is already correct — `concepts/stapp-quantum-mind` states that in Stapp's von Neumann taxonomy the agent supplies Process 1 and the *answer* is left to nature's Born-obeying choice — so this is a stranded dependent, and the fix is to inherit that disavowal at the point of use, not to re-argue it. This subsumes and sharpens the [P-Q1](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q1) sequencing note above; **both reviewers reached it independently**. (8) **Link-at-locus, exclusion**: the article denies exclusion with zero mentions of Bennett, List, Menzies, Woodward, Yablo or Baumgartner and no in-body link to `[[concepts/causal-exclusion-argument]]`, which carries the full five-response-family treatment of exactly that literature. Add the link at the sentence, and replace the two-option framing (overdetermination or epiphenomenalism) with a pointer to the three-camp map that page already contains. **Do not** write a new survey — the corpus-wide gap the reviewer alleged does not exist. (9) **Link-at-locus, closure**: the "not causally closed" sentence links only `[[concepts/causal-closure]]`; add `[[topics/causal-closure-debate-historical-survey]]` (which handles Papineau's inductive argument, Montero, Lowe and Gibb) at the locus. (10) **Named rivals**: the article's whole engagement with physicalist rivals is one parenthesis ("predictive processing, identity theories"). Name at least Laukkonen–Friston–Chandaria 2025 active inference, whose "Bayesian binding" is a selection-for-consciousness mechanism competing on the Map's own ground, linking `[[topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism]]` where the PP disagreement is asserted. Corpus coverage is fine (Laukkonen 21 live files) — this is a flagship-tier inheritance failure, not a blind spot. (11) **Ladder conformity**: the paired `topics/born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface` and `apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy` label each evidence class compatible / suggestive / discriminating; this article uses unlabelled prose. Applying the ladder per evidence class also forces defect (5) into the open, since CMD lands on "compatible, non-discriminating". Note the Claude leg **independently confirms Bodien's 25% is correct** (60/241 without observable response; 43/112 with), so item (5) stands exactly as written.

### ✓ 2026-09-07: `topics/hard-problem-of-consciousness` carries three invalid inferences (L186, L192, L196) — and L192 contradicts a register that already characterises the same datum correctly
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/hard-problem-of-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: Three separate defects in one article, all verified on disk 2026-09-06 and all in the reviewer's §11. Fix together; they are independent of each other.
  **(1) L192 — the capacity-limits inference is invalid, and the corpus already knows better.** The text reads: "the bandwidth asymmetry shows consciousness operating at a consistent ~10 bits/s throughput ceiling across all tasks—**epiphenomena do not have capacity limits, because they are not doing work**." That is false: a causally inert accompaniment can inherit limits from the capacity-limited process that produces it. **Verified at source**: arXiv:2408.10234 characterises ~10 bits/s as "the information throughput of a human being … the reduced few bits needed to control behavior" — behavioural throughput, not a consciousness channel. **The formula to copy is already in the corpus**: `positions/value-in-selection` **L61** calls the same datum "only a non-probative datum: a *behavioural* throughput ceiling measured in conscious tasks ([grain-mismatch-as-independent-evidence](/topics/grain-mismatch-as-independent-evidence/)), agnostic between the two by construction, so neither horn predicts a different figure." Align L192 to the register. The open P3 at `apex/consciousness-and-agency` also records the discipline: cite the figure "as corroboration, not as a separate result."
  **(2) L186 — the substrate-independence entailment does not hold.** "If the explanatory gap is real, [substrate-independence](/concepts/substrate-independence/) is false. Functional organization supervenes on physical arrangements—if physical facts don't explain phenomenal facts, neither does functional organization." Counterexample: psychophysical laws could pair the same experience with a given organisation regardless of substrate — nonreductive *and* substrate-independent. The claim fails **as an entailment**, independently of whether organisational invariance is true (the Map rejects it on other grounds — the causal-eligibility filter in `concepts/substrate-independence`). Fix the modal force, not the conclusion: the gap *motivates* substrate scepticism, it does not entail it. The section header "**Substrate Independence Fails**" asserts the entailment too and is a frame-level instance of the constrain-vs-establish lint in `project/coherence-inflation-countermeasures` L416 — relax the header down to the body. Note L258's Further Reading gloss ("Why the hard problem **entails** substrate skepticism") carries the same overclaim; fix all three.
  **(3) L196 — the IIT-passivity objection is a category error.** "The Map's critique adds that identity makes consciousness **passive—facing the same self-stultification objection as epiphenomenalism**." Under an identity claim the integrated information *is* the experience, so it is exactly as causally active as the neural process it is identical to; the epiphenomenalist objection does not transfer. **Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit.** Identify what kind of engagement this calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. The live objection to IIT is the one L196 already states first — that identity relocates rather than answers "why is integrated information identical to experience?" — plus the temporal-extension point. The passivity clause should go or be replaced with an argument that survives the identity reading. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body**; the classification belongs in the changelog entry, not the article. If the in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. Check whether `topics/consciousness-and-integrated-information` (the article L196 cites as "the Map's critique") makes the same move — if it does, it is in scope; if it does not, L196 is misreporting its own source.
  **PRESERVE**: L233's five-part falsification list and its closing underdetermination paragraph are the article's strongest calibration work — the reviewer's §1 singles out this kind of passage for praise. Do not touch. Length-neutral or negative; bump `ai_modified` live, hold `ai_system`; sync and grep the hugo mirror.

### ✓ 2026-09-07: The trilemma-of-selection non-exhaustiveness correction never propagated — five articles still assert the three horns are exhaustive, and a second reviewing service has now independently re-derived the finding
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: **CROSS-SERVICE CONVERGENCE — this upgrades a finding the register itself records as a singleton.** `positions/agency-and-will` L52 books the 2026-07-16 correction as "a singleton outer-review finding (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, 2026-07-16) … Not a convergent finding (single reviewer); handled as a sharpening, not a downgrade." Astra (a different service, 2026-09-06) reached the same conclusion independently — §11, "a non-exhaustive agency heuristic cannot become an exhaustive metaphysical argument further down the citation chain." It is no longer a singleton. **This is a second instance of the unpropagated-correction defect class already documented at the open [P-SC1](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc1) entry in this file** (a correction that stays local becomes a claim third parties make about the Map).
  **THE CORRECTION IS ALREADY WRITTEN — copy it, do not invent it.** Three files carry the corrected framing and are the models: `topics/trilemma-of-selection` L40 ("These three are the horns the trilemma foregrounds—a working heuristic, not a complete partition of the space"), L82, L125 ("not *forced* by the trilemma exhausting the options—as the section above concedes, it does not"); `apex/consciousness-and-agency` L78 ("The trilemma is a heuristic, not an exhaustive partition—event-causal libertarianism and sophisticated compatibilism each claim a fourth place"); `apex/embodied-interface` L99 ("rather than on an exhaustive trilemma"). **DO NOT TOUCH THESE THREE — they are correct.**
  **THE FIVE STALE LOCI, all grep-verified on disk 2026-09-06:** (1) `topics/free-will` **L84** — "at any decision point with genuine indeterminacy, **exactly three options obtain**"; (2) `topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface` **L71** — "The trilemma of selection **is exhaustive within its categories**" (note L73 of the same file is excellent and must be preserved — it fills the determinism horn with the attractor account and concedes "if that seed is exhaustively classical and thermal, the interface has no purchase here"); (3) `topics/delegation-meets-quantum-selection` **L72** — "the **exhaustive options** appear to be"; (4) `concepts/moral-responsibility` **L47** — "The trilemma of selection **makes this exhaustive**"; (5) `concepts/quantum-indeterminacy-free-will` **L77** — "sharpens this into **three exhaustive horns**".
  **A SIXTH, BORDERLINE — judge on a full read, do not blind-edit:** `concepts/selection-criterion-problem` **L43** ("The trilemma is exhaustive in the relevant sense") runs a *different*, selection-criterion trilemma (reducible-to-structure / absent / irreducible-phenomenal) whose exhaustiveness claim may be sound on its own terms; but it then imports the agency trilemma's reading via "the same way the agency-level trilemma shows its 'no fourth option' mixture inherits the costs of both." The agency-level clause is the part that is stale. Fix that clause only if the full read confirms it; leave the local trilemma alone.

Do not restate the convergent-anchor argument in five places; where a file needs more than a clause, link `[[trilemma-of-selection]]` (which argues it) or `[[positions/agency-and-will]]` (which books it). ### ✓ 2026-09-07: `topics/dualism-as-ai-risk-mitigation` — the uncomputability claim contradicts [P-Q2](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q2), the article cites the quantum-interface register nowhere, and the unbounded-magnitude argument should be replaced with a robustness frame
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/dualism-as-ai-risk-mitigation.md
- **Notes**: Three linked defects in the Map's flagship AI-facing article, all verified on disk 2026-09-06. This article is the sibling of the expand-topic above; land this first or in parallel.
  **(1) THE UNCOMPUTABILITY CLAIM CONTRADICTS THE MAP'S OWN REGISTER.** L50 and L56 argue that mind-arena consequence-distributions are "*uncomputable*, not merely intractable" — L56: "Even a Laplacean intelligence knowing every particle's position and momentum would not, by hypothesis, know what the supporting system would do next." **What that establishes is that the physical-state description is *insufficient*, which is not uncomputability** (a specific technical claim: no algorithm, any compute, any time). And the Map's own `positions/quantum-interface` **L68 ([P-Q2](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q2))** says the interaction "biases *which* outcome becomes actual within a given trial, but **the aggregate distribution over many trials matches what standard quantum mechanics predicts**." That is a fully specified, entirely computable distribution: under the Map's own default reading an AI modelling humans statistically gets the correct answer using textbook QM, by construction. **The article's existing defence at L138 misses this** — it argues empirical fragility cuts against magnitude claims while "the indeterminability sub-argument survives narrow channels intact." Channel *width* was never the issue; aggregate Born *preservation* is. A vanishingly narrow but exactly Born-preserving channel is not uncomputable in aggregate. **The replacement claim, which is defensible and does not collide with [P-Q2](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q2)**: predictive success in familiar settings does not establish that a model exhausts human subjectivity, captures everything of value, or remains adequate under unprecedented and irreversible intervention. That identifies a more credible failure mode — confidence in the wrong model class — rather than a supposed theorem that probability becomes impossible. Check whether the Leike & Hutter (2018) scaffolding at L58 still does any work under the revised claim; it currently lends technical authority the argument does not earn. The frontmatter `description:` (L3) states the uncomputability claim too and is a navigation surface — fix it in the same pass.
  **(2) THE ARTICLE NEVER CITES `positions/quantum-interface`.** Grep for `quantum-interface|mechanism-debt|P-Q2|P-Q3|born` returns **zero hits**. The convention at `positions/quantum-interface` L45 binds "any downstream article that asserts consciousness 'does causal work' and builds a practical or normative conclusion on it" — this article is the paradigm case of that use and it is uncited. Deep-link `[[positions/quantum-interface#^mechanism-debt]]`, surface [P-Q3](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q3) and [P-Q10](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q10) by name, and let the piece inherit the debt rather than reading more confident than the register upstream.
  **(3) RE-TIER THE UNBOUNDED-MAGNITUDE ARGUMENT (L100–L106) RATHER THAN DELETING IT.** L104's "low probability times potentially unbounded magnitude flips standard cost-benefit reasoning" is unsound as stated: a loss distribution can have no finite upper bound and still have tiny expected value if the tail probabilities fall fast enough. There is also an internal incoherence — the article first argues no probability distribution can be formed (L82: "there is no probability mass to spread"), then multiplies low probability by large magnitude. Those are different uncertainty regimes and cannot both be running. **Replace the expected-value framing with a robustness frame**: the live-model-set argument ("extinction is not robustly optimal across the models the agent cannot reasonably exclude"), quasi-option value under irreversibility, and the certification asymmetry — *failing to prove a bridge will collapse is not evidence it will collapse, but inability to bound a novel failure mode is adequate grounds for refusing to certify it.* **Keep the vacuum-decay illustration at L106 and the aggregate-mind-defence material, but re-tier them explicitly** into the hierarchy: core consequence (physical descriptions may omit causal variables) → natural extension (destroying mind-bearing systems could have effects not represented in the physical model) → speculative but structurally motivated (defensive arena dynamics, observer-dependent stability) → extreme illustration (radical alteration of reality). Say plainly that the extreme cases need an *additional* position — participatory ontology, idealism, observer-dependent actuality, or a cosmological psychophysical law requiring observers — and are **not deductions from Tenets 1–3**. Straightforward interactionist dualism has both domains existing and interacting; the physical world need not vanish when the mental population reaches zero.
  **(4) STRENGTHEN THE PASCAL'S-MUGGING REPLY WHILE HERE.** L104's current distinction (the unboundedness "is a feature of what dualism permits the arena to be, not a contrivance designed to elicit a transfer") is right but insufficient. The stronger distinction is that Pascal's mugging has no prior causal connection between the demanded act and the promised magnitude, so magnitude does all the work, whereas here there is an independently motivated connection — intervene on the variable your model omits and your model may fail *because* you intervened on the omission — with several structurally motivated failure modes (destroying a necessary condition, disabling a stabilising process, triggering a responsive one, destroying the only known access to the arena, destroying the possibility of learning what the omitted variable does). **Do not, however, run the hypothesis-space proposition-counting argument** in the form that counts "and X is valuable to the AI" on the upside chain but omits it from the catastrophe chain — that manufactures the asymmetry by asymmetric accounting, and the catastrophe chain needs the same step to bear on a hostile agent's decision at all.
  **PRESERVE**: L42's conditionality framing, L94's scope restriction to rational EU maximisers that recognise their model's limits, L136's coercion-substitution concession, L138's empirical-fragility concession, and L140's instrumentalism reply. These are the article's best work and the reviewer under-credited them. Length is 3883w against topics 3000 soft / 4000 / 6000 — **the article is already over soft and near the hard ceiling, so this pass must be length-neutral or negative**; the re-tiering in (3) is an opportunity to cut, not to add. `ai_modified` live `date -u`; sync; grep both trees.
